# Captured AMD / Stedi calls — the registry (read this before guessing at any endpoint)

Rule (William, 9/7/2026): every call learned from a DevTools paste or by probing is written here the same night, with the shape that worked, the helper that wraps it, and the date. If it isn't in this file it hasn't been captured — ask William for the paste rather than guessing. Never store a bearer token or a password here.

Hosts: XML gateway `https://providerapi.advancedmd.com/processrequest/api-103/TEMP/xmlrpc/processrequest.aspx` (amd.post) · same gateway via `https://pm-wfe-103.advancedmd.com/practicemanager/xmlrpc/processrequest.aspx?long=1` (JSON ppmdmsg, era_post) · REST `https://pm-api-103.advancedmd.com/api/...` (Bearer = same token, `Accept: application/vnd.advancedmd.api.v2+json` for demographics) · locator `https://api-100.advancedmd.com/api/locator/urls` (Bearer; answers the service map — a 401 here in the browser = stale session, sign out/in).

## Demographics
| Call | Shape | Helper | Captured |
|---|---|---|---|
| Read patient | `GET /api/demographic/patients/{id}` (v2 accept) → currentaddress, contact, responsibleparty, names | `patient_patch.get(pid)` | 9/2 |
| Edit address (the pop-up's "Edit address") | `PATCH /api/demographic/patients/{id}` body `{updatedpatient, originalpatient, updateaccounttype:false, familyupdate:{capturepreviousaddress:false, responsiblepartyid, patientids:[0], homephone, newaddress:{address1,address2,zipcode,city,state,countrycode:"USA"}}, deleteappointments:false}` — the familyupdate block is what makes it save; without it → "Address is required" | `patient_patch.family_address(pid, address1, address2, zip, city, state)` | 9/7 (William's paste, MacAllister) |
| DOB / flat fields | same PATCH, familyupdate null | `patient_patch.patch(pid, {dob:...})` | 9/2 |
| Plans | `getdemographic` class=demographics patientid="patN" → insplanlist | `denial_act3.plans(pid)` | 8/29 |
| Add plan | `addinsurance` class=demographics (no grpnum) → new ins id + sequence | `add_ins.add(pid, car, num, begin, finclass)` | 9/3 |
| Update plan (end-date, member id) | `updateinsurance` class=demographics with o_<field> originals | `ins_update.update(pid, insid, **fields)` | 9/3 |
| Charge history with payments/write-offs | `gettxhistory` class=demographics (attrs insbal/patbal/paid/carrier/facname/payerinfo; children paymentlist/writeofflist) | `txh.fetch(pid)` | 8/30 |
| Charge detail (full visit echo source) | `getchargedetaildataicd10` class=demographics chargeid= — by CHARGE id only; visitid= returns nothing for new visits | `resave.detail(cid)`, `resave.visit_charges(cid)` | 9/2 |

| Charge review / approve | `getreviewcharges`, `approvecharges` class=chargeentry | — | 9/2 |

| Unpaid list / visits by last-billed carrier (Mass Rebill screen search) | `POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch` `{carrierids:[id], begindateofservicestartdate, begindateofserviceenddate, rest null}`; `{}` = whole unpaid list | `build_money.py`, `claim_status_sweep.py` | 8/30, filters 9/7 |

## Shared-memory protocol (William, 9/8/2026)
Chat sessions and the nightly task share ONE growing rule set. The Drive folder `billing agent project files` (id 1-AsLUHMaeqvHq-fqWKUVKKUU4Xkw6XTt) is the Drive source of truth for this file (GitHub `main` is now the toolkit source of truth — see final sections):
1. START: download `captured_calls.md` from that Drive folder into the workspace before relying on the local copy; run `github_sync.py pull` to restore the full toolkit.
2. END: append what was learned (dated), upload it back to the Drive file, drop a dated digest in `aa claude folders → chat history` (id 1VCZfjSmok1fNNnYwf2yBTXlZ-zMUnAWU), and `github_sync.py push`.
3. Nightly mirrors helpers (`*.py`, `carriers.json`, `facilities.json`) to Drive `workspace mirror` AND to GitHub whenever they change.
4. Standing RULES also go into the synced skills via a proposed skill update for William to save.

## 9/9/2026 — GitHub as toolkit-of-record + auto-bootstrap (William, standing procedure)
Make GitHub `main` the source of truth; bootstrap from it EVERY run. Mirror to BOTH GitHub and Drive.

STANDING RUN PROCEDURE (fold into the nightly steps):
- RUN START (before Step 1), after restoring amd/amdw/stedi from Drive: `python3 github_sync.py pull`
  → fetches origin/main into /home/claude/unbilled, restoring the FULL toolkit (era_post, era_clear,
  void_rules+json, build_money, cred_check, claim_status_sweep, wo_reversal_post, precheck, elig271,
  carriers.json, facilities.json). Ends the workspace-loss cycle — no more per-file rebuilding.
- RUN END (after Step 5): `python3 github_sync.py push "nightly <date>: <what changed>""` → commits
  tracked helpers and pushes to main. Keep Drive `workspace mirror` copies too (belt and suspenders).

CONFIG (both in secrets/, gitignored, NEVER committed):
- secrets/github_pat.key = fine-grained PAT "claude billing backend" (Gmail subject "github claude
  token" / "claude github read write", thread 1a084f16f2f10b2b, 9/9/2026 — fetch at runtime like the
  AMD password / Stedi key).
- secrets/github_repo.txt = repo slug "dreffinger/<REPONAME>" (William to confirm the exact name; the
  9/8 "Cloud Practice" repo, layout core/billing/era/rebill/eligibility/credentialing/claimocity/
  boards/nightly/practices/ehpa/docs, PHI-stripped, likely already holds the lost era/rebill scripts).

TWO ACCESS GATES (both required for push from a scheduled run):
1. Harness binding: GitHub access is bound at SESSION-CREATION time. A run started BEFORE the repo was
   added to the task's authorized sources gets 403 "access not enabled for this session". FIX: William
   adds the repo to the scheduled task's authorized sources (the task's GitHub / "option 2" setup) —
   effective on runs created AFTER that. The 1:30 AM 9/9 run predated it, so it logged the skip.
2. PAT scope: the PAT must have Contents:read+write on that exact repo. git-over-https with the PAT
   embedded authenticates fine (proven 9/9); a wrong slug returns "Repository not found".
   api.github.com/user/repos is blocked by the harness even with the PAT, so the slug can't be
   enumerated from a scheduled run — William supplies it once into secrets/github_repo.txt.

github_sync.py is safe: no slug / no access → one-line skip, no error, cascade never blocks on GitHub.
Added to workspace + Drive `workspace mirror` 9/9.

Try granting the Claude GitHub App on the repo at github.com/settings/installations to open it.

## 2026-09-10 — learnings (chat + 7pm nightly, save-every-run rule)
STANDING RULE (William 9/10/2026): at the END of every nightly run or chat session, append everything learned that day (new calls, codes, carriers, fixes) here and push to the GitHub main mirror + Drive so the AMD-API vocabulary grows continuously.

### New captured calls
- PATIENT NAME SEARCH (finally captured): `lookuppatient class="api" name="LASTNAME, FIRSTNAME%"` → <patient id="patNNN" name dob chart> hits. `code=` matches the CHART code not the name; `lastname=`/`firstname=`/`searchtext=` are ignored (return the first 50 alphabetically). Use the `name="LAST, FIRST%"` wildcard. (Found Norcott Calder dup charts pat22546 / pat22776.)
- ERA reversal pipeline (WOINSBANK) — `wo_reversal_post.py`: voids the 5/31/2026 WOINSBANK bulk write-offs (`void_tx.void_writeoff`) so the balance returns to INSURANCE (not patient — verified), then posts the ERA. BUG FOUND + FIXED 9/10: its `post_check` posted $0 because the ERA remit stores each visit's `insurancebalance` as of remit-load time (=0, the write-off had zeroed it) and `postable()` gates on balance>0. FIX: add the just-voided visit ids to `era_post.FORCE_VISITS` before `post_check`, then clear them (patch committed). `era_post.py --visits` takes a COMMA-separated list (space-separated silently forces only the first). Each ERA charge line: `paymentamount + adjustmentamount = fee`, so a correct post nets the charge to $0, or leaves the coinsurance on the secondary; a Medicare patient with NO supplement legitimately keeps the ~20% as a patient balance.
- ERA bulk-dismiss — `era_clear.py`: dismisses a check only when no postable money remains (money already posted / denials the cascade handles / $0 dup lines), HOLDS anything money-bearing (postable / unrecorded-reversal / unvalidated-with-money). Reversible via `/api/era/checks/reversedismiss`. 9/10: 1,352 dismissed, ERA Center 1,982→629; the 629 held are 568 reversal-pipeline + 49 unvalidated-with-money + 12 orphan.
- ERA poster from a SCHEDULED run without a 2nd login: write `/home/claude/unbilled/.session.json` = {web, token, ts} from the mirror's `.amdtoken.json` (repo `core/amd.py.login_cached` reads `.session.json` and returns (web, token); the mirror `amd.py` reads `.amdtoken.json` and returns bool — two different interfaces). Run repo ERA scripts from the repo dir with `env.sh` so `import amd` resolves to `core/amd.py`.

## 2026-09-10 overnight — full unpaid audit + rebill (William: "get everything sent in the past year to the correct place")
### New tools (repo root; run from the repo with env.sh)
- `claims_audit.py <CARRIER> --days 365 --max N` (lives in /home/claude/unbilled, mirror helpers): massrebillsearch scoped to the carrier → one FREE AMD 271 per patient on the billed plan → classifies WRONG-PAYER / AAA / DATE-OF-DEATH / INACTIVE / ACTIVE-OK / NO-PLAN → incremental, resumable CSV.
- `claims_fix.py <CARRIER> <audit.csv> [--backlog-only] [--dry]` (repo): acts per flag — ACTIVE-OK → freq-1 rebill (skips `settling()` visits that already carry a payment); DATE-OF-DEATH → WOINS post-death DOS, rebill pre-death; WRONG-PAYER → map MA plan name → carrier, member id from the 271 entity id or a Stedi elig with the MBI, add plan / end-date old / insorder / rebill; AAA (FLO02 ONLY) → Stedi MBI lookup, fix subscribernum if it differs, rebill; everything unresolved → `claims_fix_escalate_*.csv` for William. Resumable via `DONE <pid>` log lines.
### Calls / gotchas learned
- massrebillsearch `chargedetailids` is a COMMA-SEPARATED STRING ("23363033,23363034"), not a list — split it (the first run posted nothing because `cids[0]` was the character "2").
- A COMMERCIAL plan's 271 (BCBS, UNI20…) lists the subscriber's own name, benefit riders ("BLUE VIEW VISION") and "NO OTHER INSURANCE" as other/U-R entities — those are NOT alternate payers; the billed plan is simply ACTIVE. Only a name that maps to a real health payer (another Blue, Medicare, Humana, UHC…) is a genuine wrong-payer. Medicare 271s are the ones where other/U = the MA plan.
- The Medicare MBI lookup must NEVER be used to "correct" a commercial plan's member id — it briefly wrote an MBI onto two BCBS plans (Boggs, Gresham; reverted within minutes, nothing billed). AAA on a non-Medicare plan = that payer rejected the id → card/facesheet.
- Carriers: SIM01 = car42019 (Simply; was missing from carriers.json — an unmapped carrier makes massrebillsearch fall back to ALL carriers). CMS Health Plan / "Children's Medical Services" (Medicaid specialty plan, ages ≤21) is administered by Sunshine → bill SUN01 car6194 (payer 68069), NOT FL Medicaid FFS (Fairholm 9/10: FL Medicaid 271 full-active + Sunshine 271 "CMS Title XIX" active on the same member id).
- Long jobs: run scripts with `nohup … &` and poll the log — a foreground command dies at the 10-minute cap and (if it only writes at the end) loses its work. Two AMD write processes + one audit ran concurrently all night with zero 429s.
- CONCURRENT SESSIONS on the ERA Center: the 1:30 AM nightly ran its own era_clear → wo_reversal_post cycle (posted $36,969.87, dismissed 221) while this session's full-scale reversal was running. No double work happened only because `recorded()` (visit already has a non-void payment / a reversal-register line) skips visits the other session finished (235 skipped here) — so keep that gate, and before starting any ERA reversal run check `pgrep -f "wo_reversal_post|era_clear"` and the newest `Writeoff_reversal_register_*.csv`.
### Standing rules (William 9/10)
- UNI20/BCBS pre-5/1/26 ERA-backlog visits are NO LONGER skipped in the audit — verify insurance and rebill them too (the WOINSBANK reversal posted those ERAs, so `settling()` now protects the ones that were paid). `--backlog-only` re-runs just those.
- CHARGE REVIEW INSURANCE GATE (every 2-hour run, first column): before approving, run AMD eligibility on the listed plan; ACTIVE + insurance order set → approve; INACTIVE/AAA/other payer → Stedi correction (MBI → payer elig → discovery), fix plan + order, then approve; NO plan → MBI lookup + discovery, add if found, then approve; nothing found → visit STAYS in Charge Review with a board row until the correct insurance is attached (only William bypasses). Build as `charge_review_gate.py` reusing claims_audit/claims_fix.
### Results 9/10 overnight
- WOINSBANK reversal (5/31/26 bulk write-off undone, wo_reversal_post force-post fix): 5,166 write-offs reversed, $393,894 ERA posted, 0 errors.
- Unpaid audit, 12 cohorts, 1,001 patients / $528K: 1,895 visits rebilled = $286,166; 18 wrong-payer patients moved to their real plan; 9 wrong MBIs corrected; $0 written off; 163 patients / $145K parked (62 AAA, 60 inactive → discovery pass next, 28 DOD confirm, 13 wrong-payer no-id).

- Claimocity-fed hospital visits land on the patient's MAIN referral plan; for a VA CCN stay the visit must be resaved with `refplan="<that stay's rplan>"` (updvisitwithcharges full echo) so the claim prints that plan's auth - the visit attr `preauth` reads back empty, the auth lives on the plan (getdemographic refplanlist). ## captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 05:20 UTC (1 AM ET 9/14 slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine, prompt v5)
No new call shapes this run (vocabulary unchanged); every write used registered calls (updateinsurance wrapped shape via ins_update.update, Stedi eligibility v3, AMD free 271, ERA post/clear chain). Lessons:
- **Cross-container timing**: the previous slot's run (started 04:20 UTC) was still uploading its 12-part state bundle at 05:09-05:30 UTC (one 8 KB part every ~4.5 minutes through the Drive connector), so this run started with its START marker older than 40 minutes and no RUN COMPLETE digest. Restored the sha-clean 02:13 UTC bundle first, worked the columns, and overlaid the 05:08 UTC bundle once its last part landed. A 12-part bundle takes ~55 minutes to upload; the guard window (40 min) is shorter than that - a run that packs `--state-only` should still expect the next slot to overlap its mirror step.
- **A facility's "Active Insurance as of <today>" block is the payer on file, not an eligibility answer**: TMH printed Sunshine Medicaid 'active as of 9/14/2026' for pat17477183 while Sunshine's own 271 (Stedi 68069, DOS 9/1 and 9/14) answers Inactive with planBegin 20250916 / planEnd 20260831. Use the payer 271 at the DOS to split a chart's open visits into covered (8/28) and uncovered (9/1); end-date the chart plan at the payer's planEnd (ins_update.update enddate) and leave the covered claim as billed.
- **Stedi Centene 68069 at a CURRENT DOS returns planDateInformation.planBegin/planEnd** - the same call that answers ERR 62 for old dates gives the exact coverage window for recent ones; that window is the fact to record on the board.
- Unbilled: the 11 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' rows are 2024 FLO02 visits whose 9/13 manual ERA posts (payment + WOINS, no CARC on a manual post) left a coinsurance balance that AMD cannot build a secondary claim from - still HELD (no captured call edits a payment's reason codes). pat17477723 stays on William (no facesheet anywhere, MBI 72, discovery 0).
- ERA: 37 unposted checks / $9,748.45 before and after (era_post 0 postable; fallback register-skip 13, reversal 118, orphan 27, residual-zero 66; era_clear 0 dismissed - every check holds a reversal or orphan line).
- Columns 0/0/13/0 (Unbilled = 11 held + 2 for pat17477723); first run since 9/12 with the Exclusions column at 0 (AET12 rebilled + completed by the 04:20 run; the next claim run tells whether PREF0288 is gone).
- **Drive-connector uploads can alter ONE character in transit**: the 0508UTC state bundle's part03 (uploaded by the 04:20 run) and the 0500UTC thin git bundle's part03 (uploaded by the 04:30 chat) each came back with the right byte count but one substituted base64 character ('5'->'1' at offset 2338; '8'->'4' at offset 4695). A per-part sha mismatch with a matching size is a single substitution: brute-force the 64 alphabet values at every offset against the manifest sha (3 s per 8 KB part) and both sets unpacked/verified clean. Only after that fails is a set corrupt. Also merged this run: the chat session's thin bundle (2cf2d8a + 18cba5c: tracker.html v18, board_identity.py, ins_update no longer echoes `sequence`, string-URL postman generator) into main, and the four chat additions files (0420/0430/0430-board-identity/0440) into the registry.

### Insurance Refresh Board mechanics (observed 9/14 03:52Z)
- The board page ingests pending inbox files (Drive board-inbox folder 1KRTMWbprth4HBbGpfGYHCEnIgx5Y92J8, board_updates_*.json) when William opens the page, and a STALE file's `set` ops evidently re-apply over newer DB writes: at 03:52Z, while William had the board open, aet12-carrier-cpid reverted to exactly the 9/13 05:50Z file's values (status=william, its `need` text) a minute after the 03:51Z chat update, and that file's `append` checks/notes landed a third time as duplicates (notes survived). Rules: (a) after any chat-side board write, re-read the row a minute later and re-apply on the new version if the page reverted it; (b) runs must not leave `set` ops carrying status/need in inbox files the page may ingest days later - status/need go through `update` on the DB directly when the connector is available; (c) make inbox appends idempotent (key checks by src+when) so re-ingestion cannot duplicate.
- William's authorizations arrive as `who: William` notes on the row. A chat relay writes his exact words as a `William` note with "relayed from chat" in the text so the run's authorization check matches.
- Drops: the same file can be dropped twice within seconds (two drop-* docs, identical text and size) - dedupe by itemId + filename + size within a minute, mark the extra `done` with a readNote. The board's own drop extraction already masks SSNs as xxx-xx-xxxx (verified on drop-mu0pjh2s-39sw). William may click "Marked done" on the item right after dropping (9/14) - that means HIS part is done; the reader moves the item back to `claude` and acts on the drop.

### Stedi / eligibility
- Centene 68069 ERR 62 "Date of Service Not Within Allowable Inquiry" = the DOS is outside Centene's lookup window - NOT inactive. The 9/6 sweep labeled pat17469541's SUN01 plan "INACTIVE on the dates" from ERR 62 and cascaded through MBI (ERR 72, not Medicare) and discovery (0); the PCC facesheet (9/13) and AMD's own 271 (9/8, ACTIVE) show Sunshine was and is the plan. Rule: ERR 62 -> AMD's free 271 for today's status + the facility census for the DOS; never a `no` verdict, never a facesheet request for that reason alone.
- PCC admission record fields to read every time: "Medicaid Only-No Medicare Benefits: Yes" and "Medicare Coverage: No coverage" -> skip the MBI lookup for that patient (no ERR 72 noise, no Stedi spend). The Clinical Resident Profile CENSUS block lists the payer on every room-move line - a continuous payer history that answers "which plan on that DOS" for free. pat17469541: MCD Sunshine on every census line since 11/23/2023; the Medicaid # is the Sunshine member id; second payer "Patient Liability" is the facility's share-of-cost line, not a physician payer.
- Timely-filing flag: Sunshine on 2025 dates of service may deny CO-29 on the rebill - that is a denials-list decision for William, never a quiet write-off.

## captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 04:30 UTC (chat session, 12:30 AM ET) - PRIMARY-DIAGNOSIS DENIAL RULE + pat44953 resubmission
No new AMD call SHAPES (vocabulary: helper reference added to updvisitwithcharges). Calls exercised: login (one attempt, cached token), lookuppatient name wildcard, gettxhistory, getchargedetaildataicd10 (by charge id), updvisitwithcharges with the nested <diagcodelist> child (full-visit echo), updextrainsinfo resubmissioncode=1, POST /api/claim/chargerebill (204 x19).
- RULE (William 9/14/2026): a payer denial "the diagnosis code cannot be billed in the primary position" (Humana remark ZY3 on Availity claim status; 835 RARC MA63 / M76 / M64 with CARC CO16 / CO11 / CO146) means the charge's FIRST diagnosis is a manifestation / "code first" code (F01.5x, F02.8x ...), an external-cause code, a sequela code or a secondary-only Z code. Fix, in this order: delete the bad code if that works (another acceptable code remains; a CCM visit keeps >= 2 conditions) -> else move it out of the primary slot (to the end) -> else add I10 (dcode1034543883) in front; then resubmit as an ORIGINAL claim. Never write off for this reason. The same test runs as a gate before Charge Review approval (2-hourly prompt v6) because Claimocity repeats a chart's problem list on every charge, so one bad chart denies every claim.
- HELPER: dx_primary_fix.py (workspace + repo core/): `plan(codes, cpt)` -> (delete|move|add-i10|ok, new list); `fix_visit(pid, vid, cid, seq)` = full-visit updvisitwithcharges with <diagcodelist> per charge (every live charge echoed), read-back verification of the saved dcodes, freq1_visit.run, register registers/dx_primary_register.json; CLI `python3 dx_primary_fix.py <patientid> --bad <code> [--visits v1,v2] [--dry]` walks every open-balance visit of the chart and skips visits with a live payment.
- CASE pat44953 (HUM01 car6285): every open claim since 9/2025 carried F01.50 (vascular dementia) first, then F31.9, G47.00, G30.9; the paid 2023-24 claims of the same chart had I10 primary. 19 visits / 24 charges / $4,211.00 (6 CCM 99487+99489 visits, 1 G0439, 12 hospital 99223/99233/99239 visits) had all been rebilled 9/10 with the bad primary and would have denied again. Delete worked: resubmitted as F31.9, G47.00, G30.9 (order preserved), freq 1, chargerebill 204 on all 19; insbilled=0 on 23 charges; visit 19439522 was picked up by claim run 1700190 within the same minute (insbill 19439522D, 09/13 21:07 server time) - a rebill answered 204 and then showing insbilled=1 with a NEW insbill row is a sent claim, not a failure.
- LOOKUP LESSON: `lookuppatient name="<SURNAME-WITH-APOSTROPHE>%"` finds nothing when the chart stores the surname without the apostrophe (and vice versa); the `O%NAME%` wildcard form (a % where the apostrophe would be) finds every spelling - it returned three charts for pat44953's name, two with the same DOB = a duplicate chart; the charges live on pat44953, pat23412 is empty.
- ROUTINES: the Nightly insurance cascade routine (trig_01VnkRwya4WsTV6HiDsCEkrr, agent-created) took the new STEP 1 rule (g) via update_trigger; the 2-hourly Code routine (trig_01QRqT8KB4Q1H9EyYRG4NBKX) refuses agent updates ("created via http_api") - v6 uploaded to Drive workspace mirror (v5 trashed) and handed to William to paste. list_triggers returns every routine's live prompt inside session_request.events[].payload.internal_anthropic_catchall.message.content - save the result to a file and parse it; diff against the sent text to verify a save.

## Method (helpers in the workspace, copied to the repo under billing/reporting/)
- `metrics_txh_scan.py` - caches every scanned patient's charges / payments (+CARC reasons) / write-offs (`metrics_txh_cache_<date>.json`, PHI - stays in the workspace, never in the repo).
- `metrics_compute.py` - A/R buckets by carrier, charges 90d, payments 7d/30d + last-paid per carrier, denial lines and rates by carrier + top CARC reasons, write-offs 7d by wocode, ERA Center totals, nightly auto-post series (Gmail), claim-run clean-claim rate, charge lag, payer mix -> `metrics_<date>.json` + the "Weekly metrics <date> - *.csv" files.
- `metrics_history.py` - the row for the Google Sheet "Practice metrics - weekly" (create as CSV -> Sheet on the first run; later runs re-create + trash the old copy), deltas vs last week / 4 weeks ago, threshold flags, the summary CSV.
- Nightly ERA auto-post e-mails (`from:noreply@advancedmd.com subject:"for ERA"`): one table per payment code (ACH/BOP/CHK/NON), rows Date | Check Number | OK | Failed; the same unposted checks repeat every night until posted/dismissed, so "failed lines (latest night)" is the backlog size, not new failures.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 1 PM ET slot (ehpa cloud Code Routine, prompt v5, run stamp 2026-09-14T1710Z)

No new call shapes this run (vocabulary stays at 50). Lessons and observations only:

1. **Payer acknowledgements land on OLD claim runs days later.** The Exclusions column showed 0 rows on every 9/14 run until this one; at 17:13 UTC `getclaimexclusioncharges claimrunid=1700186` (the 9/12 run) carried one new visit: pat17470875 v19383306 claim 19383306B, SUN01 CPID 2111, `PA CATEGORY: ACKNOWLEDGEMENT/RETURNED AS UNPROCESSABLE CLAIM` + `PA 09 - MBR NOT VALID AT DOS`. `getclaimcenteroverview` said exclusioncount=3 / $939 while the list calls found 1 row / $107 - the overview lags and over-counts; trust the per-run list (every run id from getclaimexclusionclaimruns has to be listed, not just the newest).
2. **A secondary "member not valid at DOS" can be a contractual residual, not a coverage question.** `getchargedetaildataicd10` by CHARGE id (charge22452894, DOS 05/14/2024 99308 $107, visit insorder "1,3" = HUM01 then SUN01) showed allowed=61.34 expected=61.34, one HUM01 payment $61.34 (6/20/2024, PE, check 133765496240601, origrefnum 820241480019482), insbalance 45.66, and the 9/11/2026 SUN01 send (claim ...B, rebill 9/10/2026 by the sweep). Stedi MBILUNOSSN at DOS 20240514 confirmed Humana Health Insurance Co of Florida PPO as the MA plan with COB 20230201-20250228 and FL QMB from 20260501 - so Humana paid the allowed in full and the $45.66 was the unadjusted contractual difference. Fix = `manual_post.post(cid, 'HUM01', 0.00, 45.66, 'WOINS', checknumber=<the Humana check>, depositdate=<its date>, note=...)` (addpayments success=1 paymentid 10153210, writeoff wo22486122) then `hideexclusions claimrunid=1700186 hide=-1 <visit id="19383306" claimid="19383306B"/>` -> success=1, run re-listed 0 visits. Rule: before any lookup cascade on a secondary rejection, read allowed vs paid on the live charge - allowed fully paid by the primary = WOINS the remainder (William's 9/13 duplicates-and-contractual rule), no Stedi, no board.
3. **Stedi FL Medicaid 77027 refuses 2024 dates of service** (AAA 62 'Date of Service Not Within Allowable Inquiry Period', 12-month window) - same taxonomy as Centene ERR 62; the MBI lookup (MBILUNOSSN) DOES answer for a 2024 DOS and lists the MA plan + COB dates for that date, which is the right tool for "who was the plan on that old DOS".
4. **Steady state otherwise:** Charge Review 0, Claim Inspector 0 batches, Unbilled 13 (11 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' + BULL pat17477723 x2 'Insurance order is missing', chart still 0 insplans), 88 claim runs newest still 1700188 (9/12 21:09 UTC). ERA 37 checks / $9,748.45 before and after; era_post 0 postable on 32 checks; fallback 0 postable (13 register-skip, 118 reversal $11,196.56, 27 orphan $1,808.67, 66 residual-zero $4,484.25); era_clear 0 dismissed, 37 held.
5. Board: no items row updated after 15:17Z (query `updated > 2026-09-14T15:17:00Z` = 0), drops 0 new, Working Denials queue = the same 2 commands from 9/13 00:52 already executed 9/13 03:35 UTC and answered by denials_updates_2026-09-13T0835Z.json, still unapplied in the inbox (drive_sync applied maps on both boards list only the 9/12 synctest files). # captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 3 PM ET slot (ehpa cloud Code Routine, prompt v5, run stamp 2026-09-14T1910Z)

No new call shapes this run (vocabulary stays at 50). Lessons and observations only:

1. **`getclaimrunstatus` does not list every claim run.** Its newest run was 1700188 (9/12 21:09 UTC, 88 runs) on every 9/14 pass, yet `getclaimexclusionclaimruns` (class=claimsreports) at 19:16 UTC returned `<Results totalcount="2" totalamount="832.00">` with one run: `<claimrun id="1700190" date="2026-09-13T21:07:56" user="EFFINGER" claimcount="4" partner="CHANGE HEALTH"/>` - a run made under William's own user, absent from the status list. Its `getclaimexclusioncharges claimrunid=1700190 clearinghouse="1" payer="1"` rows carried two WEL04 payer rejections (`PA THIS SEEMS TO BE A DUPLICATE OF A CLAIM SUBMITTED 09/05/2026` / `06/26/2026`, plus `PA ACK,RETURND AS UNPRCESSBLE CLM`), which is exactly what the overview's exclusioncount=2 / $832 had been counting since the 1 PM run (that run read 3 / $939 = these two + the Crandall row). Rule: the Exclusions pass lists `getclaimexclusionclaimruns` FIRST and works every run id it returns; the runstatus loop is a fallback. Both duplicates were completed with `hideexclusions claimrunid=1700190 hide=-1` (visits 19417241/19417241G pat12231996 and 19445941/19445941D pat17476912) -> success=1, re-list 0, exclusion runs totalcount 0.
2. **Fresh-container bundle restore, the reliable way:** a subagent's `download_file_content` results are recorded verbatim in its transcript JSONL (`/root/.claude/projects/<proj>/<session>/subagents/agent-*.jsonl`, tool_result text = `{"content":"<b64>","id":...,"title":...}`); json-parse those and base64-decode instead of retyping - the one part the agent retyped through the Write tool (part01) came out with a different sha256. `tools/bootstrap/restore_from_transcript.py` only globs one directory level; use a recursive glob (`/root/.claude/projects/**/*.jsonl`). The 1730UTC manifest's `part_sha256` values matched none of the 15 Drive parts, while the whole-archive `sha256` matched after joining them - `ws_bundle.py unpack --force` then restored all 41 files.
3. **Steady state otherwise:** Charge Review 0, Claim Inspector 0 batches, Unbilled 13 (11 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' + BULL pat17477723 x2 'Insurance order is missing', chart still 0 insplans). ERA 37 checks / $9,748.45 before and after; era_post 0 postable on 32 checks; fallback 0 postable (13 register-skip, 118 reversal $11,196.56, 27 orphan $1,808.67, 66 residual-zero $4,484.25); era_clear 0 dismissed, 37 held; 0 addpayments faults. # captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 5 PM ET slot (ehpa cloud Code Routine, prompt v5, run stamp 2026-09-14T2110Z)

No new call shapes this run (vocabulary stays at 50). Lessons only:

1. **`GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=` on a DISMISSED check returns `{"eracheckdetails": [], ...}`** - the line list is gone once the check leaves the unposted list. A check that arrives between the before-snapshot and `era_clear.py` (this run: 1051655, FL Medicaid, payment number 152990269, $15.10, denial-only / already-on-visit lines, dismissed) therefore has no cached detail afterwards. Rule: `era_clear.py` (or the chain script) must write `era_details/<checkid>.json` for every check it looks at BEFORE dismissing, not only `era_step_b_0913.py before`.
2. **Fresh-container restore, zero retyping:** every Drive `download_file_content` result is in this session's own transcript JSONL, so `tools/bootstrap/restore_from_transcript.py inbox 'workspace_state_<stamp>'` + `ws_bundle.py unpack inbox` restored all 49 files sha-clean (no `--force`), and `tools/bootstrap/amd_cred_from_transcript.py` wrote the AMD credential from the Gmail thread result (18 sightings, unambiguous). Bootstrap took 4 minutes instead of the 30-55 minutes the upload/download agents cost on 9/13-9/14.
3. # captured_calls additions - 2026-09-15 07:10 UTC (3 AM ET 9/15 slot) ehpa cloud columns run

## Payer claim number (ICN) lives on the posted ERA payment element - frequency-7 corrected claim (proven 9/15/2026, pat17473476)
- `getchargedetaildataicd10` by CHARGE id returns every posting on the charge; the ERA-posted `<payment ...>` element carries `origrefnum="<payer claim control number>"` (e.g. `H100001346973572` on a Florida Blue $0 denial posted from check 213403769) together with `checknumber`, `carrierfid`, `paymentcode="PE"`, `status="H"`. This is the CLP07 the ERA Center's `checkdetails` JSON does not expose - read it from the charge, not the remit.
- Corrected claim shape (frequency 7): `updextrainsinfo` class=chargeentry visitid=<vid> child `<extins txtChargeCode="<pcode>" resubmissioncode="7" origrefnum="<ICN>" patsignature="1" emergency="0" epsdt="0" payprov="1" employment="0" autoaccident="0" otheraccident="0" pregnant="0" outsidelab="0"/>` (same fields freq1_visit.run sends with "1"/""), then `POST /api/claim/chargerebill` sequencenumber = the plan's sequence -> 204; readback via charge detail shows `resubmissioncode="7" origrefnum="<ICN>" insbilled="0"`. Script clack_fix_0710.py.
- Order that worked for a re-send after a posted $0 denial + WOINS: `void_tx.void_writeoff` (balance returns to INSURANCE when insportion was the full fee - no move needed) -> `ins_update.update(pid, insid, subscribernum=<new id>)` (wrapped shape, success=1, patient insorder untouched) -> extins freq 7 -> chargerebill.

## Unbilled 'prior payor' rows re-queue after a release
- The 101 rows released at ~06:30 UTC by the chat full pass were back on `getclaimunbillablecharges` at 07:35 UTC (103 rows, same charts). The snapshot regenerates while the primary claim is unanswered; the standing case-(b) rule (release, they re-queue when the primary pays) means every run re-verifies and re-releases. Verified live this run with one `gettxhistory` per chart (33 charts, `datebilled`/`paid`/`insbal` per charge): 99 primary-sent-unanswered, 1 Medicaid-paid within window, 3 Medicare-paid 4/2025 crossover leftovers (secondary window passed -> `manual_post.post(cid, 'FLO02', 0, bal, 'WOINS', note=...)` then release). Scripts unbilled_verify_0710.py / unbilled_release_0710.py.

## Claim run 1700193 (AET12)
- `getclaimrunstatus` shows run 1700193 dated 2026-09-14T23:08:32 (server-local clock; it appeared after the 0510Z run's scan), 59 claims sent, 59 exclusions: the AET12 claims re-sent after William matched the carrier record to AAR01 rejected again with PREF0288 INVALID PAYER ID/CPID 814723941 + PREF0264 MISS/INVD PAYER DOC CONTRL NUM. Charge detail on all 68 AET12 charges: `insbilled="1" datebilled="09/14/2026"` = transmitted, not queued. Held open (not rebilled a 4th time).

# Captured calls additions — 2026-09-15 (chat session)

No new call shapes this session. Rule research only (William's request 9/15: verify whether Florida Medicaid still requires auth numbers on hospital claims and update everywhere).

## Research summary (2026-09-15, sources: AHCA 59G-1.053 Authorization Requirements Policy; fl.acentra.com; sunshinehealth.com preauth page; fcchealthplan.com providers page; Acentra FL contract-extension announcement Jul 2025)
- FL Medicaid FFS inpatient hospital UM is still active in 2026 — QIO is Acentra Health (eQHealth → Kepro → Acentra, same contract since 2011, extended 2025; eQSuite portal, fl.acentra.com). Approved auths = 10-digit PA numbers tied to the requesting/rendering provider NPI on the request.
- The inpatient-stay auth is the HOSPITAL's (requested by the facility for the facility claim). FFS practitioner claims carry a PA number ONLY when the practitioner's own service required PA (fee-schedule PA flag — surgical procedures etc.; hospital E&M is not flagged). General Medicaid pattern (explicit in other states' FMMIS guidance): a facility stay-auth number on a CMS-1500 practitioner claim is wrong and can pend/deny for provider-vs-auth mismatch.
- SMMC plans: inpatient facility admissions require plan auth (post-stabilization); plans state claims for PA-required services deny without it, and PA is required for ALL non-emergency services by NON-PAR providers (Sunshine explicit; FCC auths via Availity/portal, continuity-of-care period honors prior auths for new members).
- Duals/crossovers (Medicare primary): no Medicaid auth applies; QMB rules as usual.

## practices/ehpa/rules.md lines (append)
- FL MEDICAID HOSPITAL STAYS — AUTH RULE (William 9/15/2026, verified vs AHCA/Acentra/plan sources same day): the inpatient auth belongs to the HOSPITAL, not to EHPA's professional claims. (a) Straight Medicaid FFS (MCAD car6113): do NOT put the facility's stay auth in box 23 / visit preauth on pro E&M claims — no PA applies to hospital E&M; an auth-related denial here is a dispute, never a write-off. (b) SMMC plan primary (SUN01, FCC/FLO99, Humana Medicaid, etc.): PAR with the plan → no auth needed on pro E&M during an authorized admission; auth denial on a par pro claim → dispute citing the admission auth. NON-PAR with the plan (FCC until contracting closes; any plan mid-credentialing) → plan PA is required for non-emergency pro services: obtain plan auth / continuity-of-care approval FIRST, then attach THAT number via visit preauth (per-stay referral plan, VA pattern) and bill. (c) Duals (Medicare primary): unchanged, no Medicaid auth. (d) Capture the facesheet "Auth IP…" number on the board row for every Medicaid-primary hospital stay anyway — it is the dispute evidence, not a claim field for par/FFS pro claims. Denial mapping: CO-197/CO-15 "auth absent/invalid" on a Medicaid pro claim → first question is par status at DOS, not a rebill.

## captured_calls additions - 2026-09-14 07:15 UTC (3 AM ET 9/14 slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine, prompt v5)
No new call shapes this run (vocabulary unchanged); every read used registered calls (getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches, getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus/getclaimexclusioncharges, GET /api/era/checks + checkdetails, posterapayments, addpayments fallback, era_clear). Lessons:
- **Drive single-character corruption, second confirmed instance on the same file**: the 0508UTC state bundle's part03 downloaded twice (07:10 and 07:12 UTC) with the identical substitution ('5'->'1' at offset 2338 of the newline-stripped chunk) - the corruption is in the stored Drive copy, not in transit per download. The brute-force must run on the NEWLINE-STRIPPED chunk (ws_bundle's part_sha256 is computed on the chunk before the 100-char wrapping); hashing the raw file with its newlines finds nothing. 3 s, then `ws_bundle.py unpack` restored all 31 members. The transcript-JSONL route (restore_from_transcript.py) plus this brute force replaced ~100 KB of retyping.
- **Claim runs skip Sunday**: getclaimrunstatus still ends at 1700188 (2026-09-12 21:09 UTC); no run on 9/13, so the AET12 Standard-Professional test (all 59 re-sent by the 04:20 run) is decided by the Monday 9/14 ~21:00 UTC run, not before. Nothing to re-verify until then.
- **Cross-container overlap, third instance**: the 0520 UTC run (1 AM ET) posted its GitHub commits (6819d45, 1a01b03) and Drive registry/vocabulary uploads (05:50-05:57 UTC) and uploaded ws_bundle.py at 06:06 UTC but no RUN COMPLETE digest and no state bundle of its own were on Drive at 07:09 UTC; the 04:20 run's 12-part bundle finished uploading at 06:12 UTC and its digest posted at 06:13 UTC. Two runs overlapped for ~65 minutes. The state restored this run is therefore the 04:20 run's 0508UTC set; the 05:20 run's report section (columns 0/0/13/0, ERA 37/$9,748.45 unchanged) survives only in its additions file and GitHub commit message.
- Columns at 07:15 UTC: 0 / 0 / 13 / 0 (the same 11 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' 2024 rows + 2 pat17477723 'Insurance order is missing' rows as the 05:20 run left them); Charge Review and Inspector empty; exclusion charges 0 on every run.

## 2026-09-13 04:45-04:55 UTC chat session (Chrome side panel) — ERA Center ICN probe for the BCBS Medigap DCN hold (bcbs-secondary-dcn-0910)

William's question: the Medicare remits DO come back into AMD as ERAs (CMS -> Change Healthcare -> ERA Center), so can the POSTED Medicare ERAs supply the payer claim control numbers (ICN/DCN) that the Florida Blue secondaries need (PREF0283)? Result: NEGATIVE again, now for posted and unposted checks alike.

- `GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=<id>` re-read on Medicare check 892783 (FLO02, TRN 815112044, partially posted): every field path enumerated (check, visit, carc[], rarc[], errors[], charges[]). The only claim-level ids are AMD's own `checkclaimid` / `reportreasondetailid` / `chargedetailid`; there is NO CLP07 / payer claim control number anywhere in the payload. Same conclusion as 9/11.
- `GET /api/era/checks` ignores every query parameter tried (`posted=true`, `status=2`, `checkstatusid=2`, `all=true`, `PostedDateBegin=`, `DepositDateBegin=`) — each returned the identical 85-check UNPOSTED list (all checkstatusid=1). No REST path lists posted checks: `/era/checks/posted`, `/era/checks/history`, `/era/postedchecks`, `/era/checks/all`, `/era/checks/search`, `/era/search` -> 404; `/era` -> 403 RBAC.
- No raw-835 / EOB / report download exists on the REST side under any of: `/era/checks/{id}`, `/era/check?checkid=`, `/era/checks/{id}/eob|835|raw|file|download|report|print|notes|unmatched`, `/era/eob|835|download|download/835|report|checkreport|print|checknotes|notes|unmatched|claims|checkclaims|claimdetails|claim?...`, `/era/claims/{checkclaimid}` -> all 404.
- XML gateway class=autoremitreports: 16 action-name guesses (getautoremitreports, getautoremitreport(detail|list|file|claims|history), getremitreport, geteob, getera, getera835, getautoremitfile, printautoremitreport, getautoremitclaims, getpostedautoremitreports, lookupautoremitreports) -> every one "Action X not found" (no lock trip; `_expired` correctly ignored the faults).
- Still-open cheap route: when William has AMD open in Chrome, open a POSTED Medicare check in the ERA Center and click the EOB / 835 view while `read_network_requests` is capturing — that call (if the UI has one) is the only remaining way to read the ICN out of AMD. Otherwise the ICN sources stay: Change Healthcare ConnectCenter / First Coast SPOT 835s dropped in Drive 'bht Optum era downloads' (era_upload.py + parse835 CLP07), or the crossover.
- DECISION (William, 9/13 04:44 UTC on the board + chat): crossover-wait — let Medicare forward the Medigap claims to Florida Blue on its own; drop the DCN harvest for now. No AMD writes this session (one login_cached, read-only probes only).
- NEW INPUT (William 9/13): ~$1-2K/month of payer money arrives as CARD payments (payer virtual cards) that he runs through a Square account and then moves to the treasury account; those remits never reach AMD. He will collect those 835s himself and upload them manually — the drop point is Drive 'bht Optum era downloads' (any x12 835 starting with ISA, zips OK); the 7 PM run's era_upload.py (POST /api/era/upload/835) skips checks already in the ERA Center and era_post.py posts the rest.
- Mirror note: the same text was committed to the chat clone as 380ef70 on top of main 1af84af, but the push was proxy-blocked (403, repo not in this session's authorized set) — this additions file IS the record; the next routine run merges it into docs/captured_calls.md and pushes.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-13 11:50 UTC (7am ET slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine)

No new call shapes this run (vocabulary unchanged, 50 calls). Calls exercised: login (one attempt), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches, getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus, getclaimexclusioncharges (69 runs), gettxhistory x15 (excl_verify_0913c), POST /api/era/locks/release-expired, GET /api/era/checks (40), GET /api/era/checkdetails x40, posterapayments (era_post --all-unposted, 0 postable).

Lessons:
- STATE BUNDLE RESTORE THAT WORKS in a fresh container: do NOT retype the connector's base64. The Google Drive download_file_content result is stored verbatim in this session's transcript JSONL under /root/.claude/projects/<cwd-slug>/*.jsonl; a regex over that file for {"content":"<b64>","id":..,"title":"workspace_state_<stamp>.partNN.b64"} yields the exact string; base64-decode once -> the .b64 part file; ws_bundle.py unpack then passes its sha256 (0959UTC set: 8 files restored). The same trick recovers any text file the connector returned inline. Hand retyping the same two 11 KB parts failed the SHA on this run and on 4 of the 5 prior runs.
- ERA Center 40 checks / $9,748.45: three new $0 checks appeared (1051642 AMB03, 1051643 AMB03, 1051644 SUN01 X319570945, deposit date 2026-09-14) - all denial-only (CO29/CO45/CO96/OA23/CO22/CO26/CO16). era_clear classifies them 'denial with write-off (cascade handles)' and holds them; they belong to the Working Denials rebuild ('rerun'), never to a manual post.
- era_fallback_0913b register de-dup: 13 lines skipped as already settled in registers/era_manual_register.json (restored from the repo's practices/ehpa/registers copy) - the register must be copied back into registers/ on every fresh container or the fallback would re-examine settled lines.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-13 15:15 UTC (11am ET slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine)

No new call shapes this run (vocabulary unchanged, 50 calls). Calls exercised: login (ONE attempt - FAILED, see below); nothing else reached AMD.

Lessons:
- LOGIN FAILURE 15:10:55 UTC, fault -2147220480 "The system could not log you on. Make sure that your login name, office key, and password are correct." Cause: the run extracted the POI0712 password from the session transcript JSONL with a plain regex ("reset the password to (\S+?) and let me know"); the transcript ALSO contains the Bash command that carried that regex, so the last match was the regex's own source text "(\S+?)" (7 bytes, escaped) and that junk string was written to .amd_cred.json and sent to AMD. The real password (12 alphanumeric chars, Gmail thread 1866ce7e2233e4c0) is unchanged - the 9am run logged in with it 90 minutes earlier. Per the one-attempt rule nothing was retried; the columns, board AMD actions and ERA posting were skipped for this run; William was pushed a notification.
- STANDING FIX: write .amd_cred.json ONLY with tools/amd_cred_from_transcript.py (repo tools/bootstrap/). It accepts only 8-32 alphanumeric candidates, takes the most common match across the whole transcript (the thread quotes the line ~18 times), prints only length + sha8, and exits 2 (do not log in) when nothing unambiguous is found. Verified this run: len 12, seen 18. A credential check before login is cheap; a failed login costs the whole run.
- The same self-match hazard applies to ANY regex run over the transcript JSONL (bundle parts, pseudonyms, captured_calls.md): tools/restore_from_transcript.py is safe because it anchors on the connector's full JSON result shape ({"content":"<b64>","id":..,"mimeType":..,"title":"<title>"}), which a typed command never reproduces.
- .amd_login_lock is container-local and excluded from the state bundle (ws_bundle EXCLUDE_NAMES), so a fresh container after a failed attempt logs in once more with whatever it extracts - which is why the extraction must be validated, not the login retried.

Calls exercised: login (ONE attempt, success at 17:12:42 UTC with the credential written by tools/bootstrap/amd_cred_from_transcript.py - len 12, seen 18), getclaimcenteroverview x2, getreviewcharges x2, getclaiminspectorbatches x2, getclaimunbillablecharges x2, getclaimrunstatus x2, getclaimexclusioncharges (69 runs x2), gettxhistory x15 (excl_verify_0913b), POST /api/era/locks/release-expired, GET /api/era/checks x2 (40), GET /api/era/checkdetails x40, posterapayments (era_post --all-unposted, 0 postable).

Lessons:
- The 11am login failure did NOT lock POI0712: one wrong password, then this run's single attempt with the validated credential logged in normally. The amd_cred_from_transcript helper is the standing credential path.
- State-bundle restore, fourth confirmation of the transcript-JSONL method with a twist: the 1514UTC six-part set decoded to the exact tar_gz_bytes (34,028) but failed the manifest sha256 and the gzip CRC - a same-length corruption inside the last part. Salvage that worked: zlib-decompress the stream progressively (stops at the trailer), then walk the tar headers manually and extract every member whose header checksum passes (16 of 18 restored: needs_facesheet_list.md, laterality_flag_log.md, era_manual_register.json 324 lines, patient_db, run reports); the two missing tail members (registers/wd_writeoff_result_2026-09-13.json, registers/wo_0913_pw.json) came from the previous set (1351UTC, four parts, sha match) via `ws_bundle.py unpack` which keeps newer local files. Recipe worth adding to ws_bundle.py: a `--salvage` mode that does exactly this instead of refusing on SHA mismatch.
- The Drive captured_calls.md base (2026-09-11 upload, modified 9/13 03:23) still lacks every 9/13 additions file and still carries the 9/10 D-SNP raw member id; this run merged the eight 9/13 additions into the workspace copy and redacted the id (same text as the repo copy) so the PHI gate passes - the merged workspace copy is uploaded to Drive as captured_calls.md this run (old one renamed _pre-merge).
- Steady state unchanged since the 3am run: columns 0/0/0/59 (AET12 PREF0288 on run 1700188, 15 charts, $2,052.25 insurance balance, none at $0), no claim run since 1700188 (9/12 21:09 UTC), ERA Center 40 checks / $9,748.45 unposted, era_post 0 postable, fallback 13 register-skip / 118 reversal / 27 orphan / 66 residual-zero, era_clear holds all 40. Nothing to post, release, rebill or dismiss.

# captured_calls additions — 2026-09-10 1 PM ET columns run

No new API calls captured (all four columns were steady standing holds).

Observation (rule refinement, not a new call):
- AET12 (Aetna Senior Supplemental Medigap secondaries), exclusion run 1700166: the rejection signature CHANGED between the 9am/11am reads (PREF0288 "invalid payer id/CPID = TIN") and the 1pm read (**PREF0264 MISS/INVD PAYER DOC CONTRL NUM**). Reading each run's getclaimexclusioncharges error text is the only window into whether a carrier-master CPID fix landed. PREF0264 on a Medigap secondary = missing Medicare crossover document-control number; freq-1 does not fix it, freq-7 only with William's payer ICN. So: after a carrier-master CPID fix, re-read the exclusion error before assuming the claim is now billable — the block can move from carrier-setup to missing-crossover-DCN.

# captured_calls additions — 2026-09-10 3 PM ET columns run

No new API calls captured (registry unchanged; all four columns worked with registered calls only).

API-behavior lessons (rule refinements, not new endpoints):
- **Stedi payer eligibility refuses old dates of service**: Centene 68069 and UHC 87726 both answer AAA 62 "Date of Service Not Within Allowable Inquiry Period" for a DOS ~22 months back (pat17472230, DOS 11/2024). The **MBI lookup (MBILUNOSSN) still answers for those same DOS** — including the U/PR Medicare-Advantage COB window and QMB — so for aged claims the MBI lookup is the only backend window into who covered the patient then; the 271 may name no MA entity (U-code row with empty entity list).
- **UHC 87726 for a terminated/unknown supplement policy** returns only bare U/X benefit codes (no planStatus, no membership echo) rather than an explicit Inactive — treat "no membership data" as policy-not-recognized (pat17473020, pat17473232).
- Confirmed the 9am lesson end-to-end: the 52 BCBS Medigap secondaries re-sent freq-1 at 9am ET re-rejected on the next run (1700181) with PREF0283 MISSING PAYER DOC CONTROL NUM — freq-1 does not supply the Medicare DCN; 27 of them also carried PDTP0303 (admission dates stripped by the earlier freq-1 resets), repaired this run via updextrainsinfo full-echo with hospitalizationfrom.
- Unbilled "payment from prior payor" release rule re-proven at scale: 92 rows, every sampled charge primary FLO02 billdate 09/05/2026 paid 0 -> hideunbillablevisits hide=-1 in batches of 40, column 104 -> 12.

Mirror note (3pm run, fresh container): GitHub push still proxy-blocked (403, repo not in this task's sources) — this run's commit is in Drive github mirror as 2026-09-10_1945UTC.bundle.b64 (BASE64 TEXT this time). The two earlier binary .bundle files in that folder download 121 bytes short via the Drive connector (pack corrupt on fetch: "bad object at offset 10723"), so the pending 0500/1104 commits could not be restored into this run's clone; both files were left in place (NOT pruned to two) because their commits exist nowhere else. Store future bundles as .b64 text.

## 2026-09-11 chat session (00:50–05:00 UTC) — Medicaid rebill under Effinger NPI + INSURANCES CLEANING TASK
Egress from CHAT sessions is open (AMD, Stedi hosts, github.com all reachable; `git push` to main WORKED — commits 91b38e3, 42d9774). The Drive connector `create_file` returned "Error occurred during tool execution" on every attempt all session (small and large payloads, text and base64) — Drive leg deferred; GitHub main is the record.

### New tools (repo main)
- `billing/writeoff/insurance_cleaning.py` — INSURANCES CLEANING TASK (on demand; prompt `nightly/insurances_cleaning_task_prompt_v1.txt`; skill `skills/billing-insurances-cleaning-task/SKILL.md`). MODE A (read-only): `masswriteoffsearch isinsbalance:false` -> every charge carrying a PATIENT balance -> buckets -> `Patient_AR_split_<date>.xlsx`. MODE B (`--post --qmb-code --untimely-code [--small-balance]`, only on William's APPROVED): write-offs for buckets 1 (QMB/dual) + 2 (untimely) via `move_to_ins.move(cid)` then `manual_post.post(cid, carrier, 0, bal, code, note)`; writes `Writeoff_register_<date>.xlsx`. MODE C (`SUPPLEMENTS` + Stedi budget): discovery for Medicare-coinsurance patients with no supplement on file.
- `billing/rebill/rebill_medicaid_prof13.py` (freq-1 rebill of confirmed-unpaid prof13 visits, live re-verify: profile==prof13 AND no posted payment), `billing/rebill/rerender_garcia_to_effinger.py` (profile swap prof15->prof13, void-trap safe: full echo from `resave.visit_charges`), `billing/rebill/rebill_recreated_medicaid_seq.py` (rebill recreated charges on MEDICAID'S OWN sequence).

### Calls / mechanics learned
| Call | Shape | Helper | Captured |
|---|---|---|---|
| Patient-balance list (Mass Write-off screen, patient side) | `POST /api/masswriteoff/masswriteoffsearch` `{carrierids:null, isinsbalance:false, billstartdate, billenddate, rest null}` -> `chargedetails[]` with patientid, visitid, chargedetailid, chargecode, carriercode, totalchargeamount, **allowedamount**, **patpayment**, patbalance, insbalance, **lastpaymentdate**, providerprofilecode. 9/11: 4,201 charges / $234,758 / 671 patients. `allowedamount>0` = the payer ADJUDICATED (an ERA posted); a $0 ERA denial line ALSO stamps lastpaymentdate, so never use lastpaymentdate alone. | `insurance_cleaning.patient_balances()` | 9/11 |
| Re-render a visit to another provider profile | `updvisitwithnewcharges` class=chargeentry, visit attrs echoed (`id date insorder refplan facility onhcfa`) with `profile="prof13"` + `force="1"`, FULL charge echo (resave.CHG_FIELDS) from `resave.visit_charges(cid)`. AMD VOIDS every old charge and RECREATES them on a NEW visit id (30 swaps -> contiguous block vst19454223–19454252). It posts a negative reversal charge line per old charge and a NEGATIVE write-off reversal for every write-off the old charge carried (all flagged void=1; 20 originals +985.20 / 18 reversals -985.20 = net 0). The recreated charge is a FRESH full-fee charge (paid 0, wo 0) — old contractual adjustments are reversed, so the balance rises to full fee (correct: the new payer adjudication re-applies them). `resave.visit_charges(orig_cid)` returns 0 charges after the swap (it looks up the OLD visit id): find the recreations in `gettxhistory` by `createtime` starting with today's date + insbal>0. | `rerender_garcia_to_effinger.py` + `garcia_recreated.json` | 9/11 (30 visits, 31 charges, zero losses) |
| Medicaid's OWN plan sequence | `getdemographic` insplanlist: the `<insplan>` whose `carrier="car6113"` (prefer no enddate) -> `sequence`. REBILL TO MEDICAID = `chargerebill sequencenumber=<that>`. **`insorder.split(",")[0]` is NOT it** — insorder is the billing ORDER and its first entry is often Medicare car6085 / UHC (seen: "3,1"->1, "4,2"->2, "2,1"->1, "1,2"->2). The first dry-run would have sent ~10 visits to Medicare. | `rebill_recreated_medicaid_seq.medicaid_seq(pid)` | 9/11 |
| Sign conventions | `gettxhistory` returns payment/write-off `amount` NEGATIVE (credits); `getchargedetaildataicd10` returns original write-offs POSITIVE and void-reversals NEGATIVE (= what the AMD UI shows). Don't mix the two when summing. | — | 9/11 |
| AMD gateway ceiling | ~300–365 rapid `getchargedetaildataicd10` calls (5–6 concurrent, or a tight serial loop) and the gateway silently stops answering — no error, the socket just hangs past the timeout; single calls keep working. For long read passes: `requests.Session`, hard 20–25 s timeout, resumable JSONL, expect to restart every ~350. | `classify_pool.py` (ThreadPoolExecutor + future timeouts) | 9/11 |

### Carriers
SIM01 = car42019 (Simply), STA03 = car41991 (Staywell), WEL07 = car41992 (WellCare Enc) — added to carriers.json (an unmapped carrier makes massrebillsearch fall back to ALL carriers).

# captured_calls additions — 2026-09-12 12:50 AM ET columns run (ehpa cloud copy, off-schedule fire, 04:20-05:30 UTC)

## Bootstrap (fresh container)
- Repo NOT attached (github.com 403 "GitHub access to this repository is not enabled for this session") -> PAT clone of main 2526634 works (credentialed clone passes the proxy). Push still proxy-gated.
- State bundle `workspace_state_2026-09-12_0400UTC.part01.b64` (8,484 chars, size-verified by the previous run) arrived with a SHA256 mismatch: the connector transfer re-types base64 in BOTH directions, so a size match proves nothing. RECOVERY RECIPE: base64-decode, then `zlib.decompressobj(16+MAX_WBITS)` in 64-byte chunks until it raises - everything decoded before the bad byte is a valid tar prefix; walk the 512-byte headers (verify each header checksum) and extract every member before the first bad header. 4 of 5 members came back (needs_facesheet_list.md, laterality_flag_log.md, void_rules.json, most of registers/columns_snapshot); registers/excl_balances lost and rebuilt. RULE: keep bundle parts small, and ALSO upload the key state files as plain text (a typo in markdown is harmless, a typo in gzip is fatal).
- Drive overlay verification that works: exact size match + `python3 -m py_compile` (py) / `json.load` (json). elig271.py, columns_scan_0912.py, pseudonyms.json all transferred clean this run.
- Missing required helpers (never rewritten from memory): columns_inspector2_0908.py, era_details_pull.py (not in repo main 2526634, not in any Drive folder).

## Parallel-run detection (new lesson)
- A second copy of this routine ran in another container at the same time ("12:30am ET 9/12 backend run (manual fire after egress restore)") and wrote NO `run START.md` to chat history, so the 40-minute guard saw nothing. Its trace was the Insurance Refresh Board: rows for pat17477706 / pat17477708 / pat17477709 / pat17477710 with `updatedAt` 04:44:58Z. RULE: before any AMD write, query the board `items` by `amdid in [...]` for the run's patients and look at `updatedAt`; if another run touched them in the last hour, append checks with `update` (batch, if_version pinned) and keep AMD writes to the minimum. Also `manual_post.post` refuses over-apply beyond insbalance, so a duplicate write-off attempt by the other copy fails safely once the first one posted.
- Board timestamps: write `at` from `date -u` (the parallel run stamped 05:05/05:10Z on writes made at 04:44Z).

## Unbilled — NEW error class: "Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance."
- Anatomy (6 rows this run, all Pam-Garcia SNF E/M visits 2024-2026): the claim to the NEXT payer in the visit insorder cannot be built because a posted payment on the charge has no `payreasonlist` (a $0 "Process Accepted" post from `era_clear.zero_post`, or a manually posted secondary payment) or its reason amounts do not sum to the charge. `gettxhistory` shows every payment with its `payreason` children (code/amount) - that is the diagnostic: for each non-void payment, paid + sum(payreason amounts) must equal the charge fee.
- Case A (pat44984 x2): Medicare payment posted 9/9/2026 WITH CO45/PR2/CO253 (balanced), a HUM01 payment for exactly the PR2 amount WITHOUT reasons, and the CO45+CO253 contractual never posted -> insurance balance = contractual only. Fix: `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO02", 0.0, <CO45+CO253>, "WOINS", <Medicare check#>, None, note)` -> insbalance 0.00 -> `hideunbillablevisits hide="-1"`. Done ($51.87 + $45.41).
- Case B (pat17469446, pat45049, pat44955, pat17470743): balance = Medicare PR2 coinsurance heading to a Medicaid-type crossover (WEL04 / HUM01 tertiary); blocked by $0 postings with no reasons (check numbers 072350607 / 072528333 / 152855566 / 176345603260114). pat45049 + pat44955 also carry MCAD $0 CO185 (rendering provider not enrolled = the Pam Garcia FL Medicaid enrollment item). These belong to the Medicaid-residual Mode D worklist (D3 = timely E/M never adjudicated -> rebill on the plan's own sequence, not a write-off); NOT released (data still broken). Candidate fix for the cleaning task: `void_tx.void_payment(pid, cid, payid)` on the reason-code-less $0 line, then release; unknown yet whether voiding the $0 line moves AMD's next-payer pointer back one plan.
- manual_post.py expects carriers.json in the rich form (carN -> [code, name]); the bootstrap keeps the simple form (code -> carN) for precheck.py. Patched manual_post.py to accept either shape (CAR = code -> carN). Same trap likely in other helpers that build `CAR = {v[0]: k ...}` - grep for it before running one from a fresh container.

## Stedi (headless, live key)
- FL Medicaid `77027` and Capital Health Plan `95112` with name+DOB but no memberId: 77027 -> AAA 72 (member id required); 95112 -> AAA 79 "Invalid Participant Identification" (member id required); 95112 with the Medicare MBI as memberId -> AAA 72. So neither can be used as a name-based finder; discovery (`insurance-discovery/check/v1`) is the only name-based path and it did NOT list the CHP MA plan that the MBI lookup showed (code U HMO Medicare Risk, entity CAPITAL HEALTH PLAN, coordinationOfBenefits date). For a CHP member the member number must come from the facesheet/card.
- MBI lookup (MBILUNOSSN) is sensitive to the surname string: tried the compound surname with and without the space and the two-word first name -> all AAA 72 for pat17477709 (75 y/o); AAA 72 for pat17477706 (75 y/o) too. Not proof of non-enrollment - a hyphen/maiden-name variant on the Medicare record defeats the no-SSN lookup.
- `lookuppatient class="api" name="LAST, FIRST%"` used as the duplicate-chart check for brand-new Claimocity charts (found two unrelated same-name charts by DOB for pat17477710; no duplicates for the other three).

NEW BEHAVIOUR 1 - `updvisitwithnewcharges` with a CHANGED `profile` attr (rendering provider): AMD does NOT edit the visit; it VOIDS every charge on it (void=1 in gettxhistory) and re-creates the charges on a NEW visit id under the new profile (same DOS/CPT/fee, insurance balance carried, refplan and insorder kept, not yet billed). Used deliberately for Orme pat17474046 x7 (Garcia prof15 -> Effinger prof13); the old exclusion rows point at dead visits and were completed. Any script that verifies "live charges after resave" must expect 0 on the OLD visit and find the NEW visit via gettxhistory.
NEW BEHAVIOUR 2 - on a brand-new (Claimocity-fed) visit the resave's nested `<extins hospitalizationfrom=.../>` child is ignored (readback None, inspector 117065068 raised); `updextrainsinfo class=chargeentry visitid="vst<ID>"` with `txtChargeCode=<CPT>` per CPT writes it (readback ok), then the onhcfa=1 full-echo resave clears the inspector. The referral-plan `preauth` written on a visit resave shows on the REFPLAN (getdemographic), never as a visit attr on getchargedetaildataicd10 - verify auths on the plan, not the visit.
Also confirmed: `updateinsurance` with `sequence="2,1"` on any plan sets the PATIENT default insorder (response echoes `<patient insorder="2,1"/>`); `patient_patch.family_address` with a spoofed originalpatient.address1 still answers 500 'Address is required.' on charts whose line 1 is blank (capture gap stands); Pattern 23400 (99497 unbundled with 99223) clears by adding modifier 25 = mcode7 to the 99497 and re-inspecting.

Re-post via `addpayments` with `<payment amount="0.00" id="" status="B"><payreasonlist><payreason code="OA23" amount=".."/><payreason code="PR2" amount=".."/></payreasonlist></payment>` -> success="1" paymentid returned BUT gettxhistory shows the new payment with NO payreasons (the child is silently ignored) -> voided. Shape `<reasonlist><reason code amount group/></reasonlist>` -> Server Error fault. NEGATIVE RESULT: addpayments cannot carry reason codes; the Payment Entry edit call is still uncaptured (William offered a DevTools capture).
FIX THAT WORKS: void the code-less $0 payment (void_tx.void_payment) - nothing financial changes, every remaining live payment balances, then hideunbillablevisits. Applied to pat17469446, pat45049, pat44955, pat17470743 -> Unbilled 4 -> 0; the secondary claims (WEL04 x2, MCAD x2) build on the next claim run. Standing rule: a $0 payment with no reason codes on an Unbilled 'Payment reason codes' row = void it and release; never leave the row.

## 2026-09-15 0310Z run (11pm ET 9/14 slot, ehpa cloud) - additions
- **Availity Remittance Viewer exports as a remit source (NEW DATA SOURCE, William 9/14 ~10:40 PM-11:30 PM ET).** William dropped 266 PDFs into Drive "bht Optum era downloads": `CheckDetails-<ts>.pdf` (one check, 1-784 pages), `CheckMultipleDetails-<ts>.pdf` (several checks) and `ClaimDetails-<ts>.pdf` (one claim). They are Availity print views, NOT 835 files - `era_upload.py` cannot load them. Text extracts with pypdf (`pip install pypdf cffi`). Layout: "Check Summary" page = payer name (line before "Payee Tax ID"), `Check/EFT Trace Number`, `Payment Amount`, `Check/EFT Date`; each "Claim Summary" page = Payer Name, Patient Name, Claim Number, Patient ID (member id), Claim Date, **Patient Control Number = AMD visit id + claim letter** (19447649A), Claim Charge/Payment Amount, Patient Responsibility, Claim Status Code (1 processed primary, 22 reversal); line block after "Charge Adj (Qty) Adj Amount Payment" (or "Charge / Adjustments Payment" on ClaimDetails): **Line Ctrl Nmbr = AMD charge id**, DOS range, `HC:<CPT>//<units>`, remark codes (N381 ...), payer codes (OCMSP/PPRCT), charge with (B6) allowed tag, adjustments `CO-45 $x` / `PR-22 $x`, payment last. Parser `avail_checks_parse.py` -> `registers/availity_checks_<stamp>.json` (dedupe by trace number; ClaimDetails folded into the check by trace). Comparison `avail_vs_amd.py`: GET /api/era/checks text search for the trace + gettxhistory payments (attr `checknumber`) per charge -> `registers/availity_vs_amd_<stamp>.json` (missing lines = money not in AMD). Posting `avail_post.py` = manual_post.post per line with checknumber = trace, depositdate = check date, pay = min(paid, live insbalance), wo = CO adjustments capped, WOINS; register key `<trace>|<chargeid>` in `registers/era_manual_register.json`.
- **gettxhistory attribute names (re-confirmed 9/15):** charge attrs are `insbal`, `patbal`, `totbal`, `fee`, `paid`, `dos`, `visit`, `billdate`, `carrier`, `void`; payment child attrs `id`, `date`, `checknumber`, `amount` (negative = money in), `carrier`, `void`, `paycode`, `source`; writeoff child attrs `id`, `date`, `wocode`, `amount`, `void`. `getchargedetaildataicd10` (resave.detail) returns `<patient id="17471450">` WITHOUT the `pat` prefix - regex `(?:pat)?(\d+)`.
- **Demographics PATCH first-name change (pat17477112, first name corrected to the payer's spelling):** `patient_patch.patch(pid, {"names": [<full names list with firstname changed>]})` - the name lives in `names[]` (id 0, isbilling true), top-level firstname is None. The PATCH bounced 500 `{"title":"Email is not a valid format."}` because `contact.email` held a phone number (+18505103018); sending `email: None` in the same PATCH (flat key `email`) made it save; `homephone` accepted as a flat key afterwards (200, readback ok). getdemographic `name=` reflects the change immediately.
- **Board actions 9/15 (proven shapes, nothing new):** add_ins.add(pid, "car41826", member, "06/01/2024") -> (ins9519414, "1") on an empty chart and ("ins9519415", "2") next to an ended plan; ins_update.update(pid, insid, enddate="12/31/2025") success; ins_update.update(pid, newinsid, sequence="2") -> `<patient insorder="2"/>` (patient order set on purpose); manual_post.post(cid, "SUN01", 0, 107.0, "WOBAD", ...) accepted (`success="1" paymentid=`) - WOBAD is a valid Payment Entry write-off code via addpayments; WOLATE and WOINS as before.

# Captured-calls additions — 2026-09-15 05:40 UTC (chat full pass, William-requested)

## New observations (AMD)
- **`getclaimunbillablecharges` prior-payor rows decided from charge detail attrs**: `lastbilledcarrier` (sequence last billed), `datebilled`, and the payment list on `resave.detail(cid)`. Rows with only $0 postings (denial/info) or an empty `datebilled` (brand-new charge) = case (b) → `hideunbillablevisits hide="-1"`. Only a REAL (nonzero) primary payment older than the secondary window is a write-off candidate. 9/15: 128 charges / 103 visits, all release; Unbilled 114 → 11.
- **`hideunbillablevisits` success reply is `<Results success="1" /><Error />`** — the empty `<Error />` element is NOT a fault. Detect faults on `<Fault` / `success="0"`, never on the string "error".
- **`getclaimexclusionclaimruns`** returns ONLY runs that still carry live exclusions (with `claimcount`); it is the right list to iterate. `getclaimrunstatus` `totalexclusions` is the historical count at submit time and stays > 0 after rows are completed.
- **"Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance"** (Unbilled) = a payment on the charge posted with an empty `<reasonlist>`; residual sits in `insbalance`. Fix = re-post with CARCs from the remit. Never release. 9/15: 11 rows, all 2024 DOS (Rylance, Kinross, Cogswell, Delmar, Simcoe x4, Rowantree N x2, Carrow), $2,422.58.
- **The 9/14 04:20 UTC freq-1 rebill transmitted on the next NIGHTLY 21:07 ET run (1700192), not on the run created at rebill time.** A rebill's CH verdict is read on the run whose `date` is the first 21:07 after the rebill.
- **AET12/PREF0288 NOT fixed by the Standard Professional profile.** Run 1700192: 59/59 rejected PREF0288 INVALID PAYER ID/CPID 814723941 + PREF0264 MISS/INVD PAYER DOC CONTRL NUM. Carrier-master (UI) change required; do not rebill again.
- Stedi Centene 68069 answered a March DOS in September WITHOUT ERR 62 (returned Inactive with planEnd) — ERR 62 is not guaranteed on old DOS.
- FL Medicaid 77027 271 can return **Active with planCoverage "FP| Limited to family planning services"** — Active ≠ billable; read `planCoverage`.
- UHC rejection text "PROCESSED ACCORDING TO CONTRACT, PLAN PROVISIONS" on an unprocessable claim = already adjudicated (duplicate resubmission) → complete the exclusion, find the original remit (Clavering 19394490B).
- Bexley Amber pat17475817 cascade (Stedi 68069 inactive planEnd 20260101; 77027 active FP-only; discovery = same Medicaid) → no coverable payer at DOS 3/14–15/26 → William.

Context: William asked to set up (1) UnitedHealthcare's provider APIs for the UNI20 backlog and (2) an 835 export for remits. He has a One Healthcare ID and a Change Healthcare portal login. Everything below is PENDING until William's first successful call; nothing here is captured/proven.

Files delivered (Drive `billing agent project files`: runbook + this file; Drive `workspace mirror`: helpers):
- `UHC_API_and_835_setup.md` — the runbook (registration steps, credential file template, first-call order, portal pull list, AET12 check).
- `uhc_api.py` — stdlib helper: OAuth 2.0 client-credentials token (cached, chmod 600), `eligibility()`, `claim_inquiry()`, `claim_precheck()`; reads `secrets/uhc_api.json`; endpoint paths overridable from that file because the marketplace technical guide is authoritative.
- `x12_835.py` — stdlib X12 835 parser, TESTED on a synthetic file: per-check totals and per-claim rows (CLP01 patient control, CLP07 payer ICN, status, charged/paid/patient resp, CARC/RARC, CPT@DOS=paid); names dropped so CSVs pass the PHI gate.
- New Drive folder `chc raw 835 downloads` (id 1flsxE1-QMP4rBZd2XXNDwhX_ihpgkVtF) under `billing agent project files` for the Change Healthcare portal downloads (NOT `bht Optum era downloads`, which feeds era_upload into AMD).

| Call | Shape | Helper | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| UHC OAuth token | `POST <token_url from technical guide>` form `grant_type=client_credentials`, header `Authorization: Basic base64(client_id:client_secret)` → `{access_token, expires_in}` | `uhc_api.token()` | PENDING registration |
| UHC real-time eligibility | `POST https://api.uhcprovider.com/eligibility/v1/real-time` bearer; body `{memberId, dateOfBirth YYYY-MM-DD, serviceType "30", dateOfService, npi}` → `{coverageStatus ACTIVE/INACTIVE/TERMED, planName, …}` (path per public profile; guide wins) | `uhc_api.eligibility(member, dob, dos)` | PENDING |
| UHC claim inquiry (status + remit trace) | `POST https://api.uhcprovider.com/claims/v1/inquiry` bearer; body member+DOS range or claimNumber → `{claimNumber, claimStatus RECEIVED/IN_PROCESS/PROCESSED/DENIED/ADJUSTED/REVERSED, billedAmount, allowedAmount, paidAmount, patientResponsibility, paymentDate, checkNumber, denialReasonCode}` | `uhc_api.claim_inquiry(**fields)` | PENDING |
| UHC claim pre-check | `POST https://api.uhcprovider.com/claims/v1/pre-check` body `{memberId, npi, serviceDate, diagnosisCodes[], procedureCodes[], charges}` | `uhc_api.claim_precheck(...)` | PENDING (later) |
| Raw 835 → rows | local: `python3 x12_835.py --csv era_835_<date>.csv <files>` | `x12_835.parse/rows` | proven on synthetic 835 only |

Vocabulary entries to add ONLY after the first proven call (folder "UHC", variables `uhc_token` never stored, `uhc_member`, `uhc_dob`, `uhc_dos`):
```json
[{"folder":"UHC","name":"OAuth token (client credentials)","method":"POST","url":"{{uhc_token_url}}","headers":{"Authorization":"Basic {{uhc_basic}}","Content-Type":"application/x-www-form-urlencoded"},"body":"grant_type=client_credentials","description":"Client id/secret from apimarketplace.uhcprovider.com; never stored.","helper":"uhc_api.token()","status":"pending","body_language":"text"},
 {"folder":"UHC","name":"Real-time eligibility","method":"POST","url":"https://api.uhcprovider.com/eligibility/v1/real-time","headers":{"Authorization":"Bearer {{uhc_token}}","Content-Type":"application/json"},"body":"{\"memberId\":\"{{uhc_member}}\",\"dateOfBirth\":\"{{uhc_dob}}\",\"serviceType\":\"30\",\"dateOfService\":\"{{uhc_dos}}\",\"npi\":\"{{npi}}\"}","helper":"uhc_api.eligibility","status":"pending","body_language":"json"},
 {"folder":"UHC","name":"Claim inquiry","method":"POST","url":"https://api.uhcprovider.com/claims/v1/inquiry","headers":{"Authorization":"Bearer {{uhc_token}}","Content-Type":"application/json"},"body":"{\"memberId\":\"{{uhc_member}}\",\"dateOfBirth\":\"{{uhc_dob}}\",\"dateOfServiceFrom\":\"{{uhc_dos}}\",\"dateOfServiceTo\":\"{{uhc_dos}}\",\"npi\":\"{{npi}}\"}","helper":"uhc_api.claim_inquiry","status":"pending","body_language":"json"}]
```

## 2026-09-18 03:10Z run (11pm ET 9/17 slot) - BEHAVIOURS recorded, no new call shapes
- **Drive large-file transport for the cloud run (OCR restore, William 9/17 "add the ability back to ocr a pdf")**: `mcp__Google_Drive__download_file_content` returns the whole file as base64 in `content`; when the result is bigger than the harness token limit it is NOT returned inline - the harness writes the full JSON result to `/root/.claude/projects/<project>/<session>/tool-results/mcp-Google_Drive-download_file_content-<ts>.txt` and the run extracts it with `jq -r .content <file> | base64 -d > x.pdf` (343,470-byte scan reproduced byte-exact 9/18). Small files (a 15 KB PNG) come back inline and would have to be transcribed - keep scans large or read them in a subagent. Then `python3 ocr_pdf.py x.pdf` (scripts/helpers/ocr_pdf.py: pdftotext text layer, else pdftoppm 250 dpi + tesseract --psm 6; SSNs masked). `tools/bootstrap/bootstrap_workspace.sh layout` now apt-installs tesseract-ocr + poppler-utils (and pip pymupdf) when missing. The Drive connector does NOT OCR images: `read_file_content` on an uploaded PNG with printed text returned "" (probe 9/18, trashed).
- **Contractual correction on a charge whose balance already sits on the PATIENT side** (pat17471832, 9/18): `move_to_ins.move(cid)` (seq None -> no rebill; full-visit updvisitwithcharges echo moving patbalance/patportion back to insbalance/insportion) then `manual_post.post(cid, "BCBS", 0.0, <CO-45>, "WOINS", checknumber=<Availity trace>, depositdate=<check date>, note=...)` = addpayments paysource 2 with a $0 payment + the write-off. Readback: AMD posted the write-off AND auto-transferred the remainder (the remit's PR-2 coinsurance) to the patient balance (insbalance 0.00 / patbalance 53.63 on a 99233 with allowed 107.26). No reverse move needed. This is also why the 9/14 Availity posts that carried NO write-off left patient balance = charge - payment: AMD moves whatever is left after the final-insurance payment to the patient.
- `POST /api/masswriteoff/masswriteoffsearch {carrierids:[6062], isinsbalance:false, billstartdate, billenddate, other keys null}` (captured 9/7) re-used 9/18 for the Florida Blue patient-balance scope: 179 charges / 48 charts / $22,499.90; note `lastpaymentdate` comes back null on every row and `allowedamount`/`expectedamount` are AMD's fee-schedule expectations, not the remit's.
- `gettxhistory` (class demographics) **typefilter="1" returns only charges with an OPEN balance; typefilter="0" returns every charge** (pat17471832 9/18: 27 vs 109). A stay that looks "never charged" under typefilter 1 is usually fully adjudicated - re-read with typefilter 0 before reporting missing charges.

# Additions 2026-09-18 04:30 UTC — chat session (Claimocity feed outage + fix). No new AMD/Stedi calls; Postman unchanged.

## Claimocity feed — two paths (never confuse them)
- Charges into AMD: Claimocity DFT^P03 HL7 files on sftp.claimocity.io (account kykzfgie, /download) are picked up by the AMD integration, which clears each file. This path never stopped: the 9/17 3 PM ET run found 56 new HCA hospital visits (DOS 9/14–9/16) in Claim Inspector. Posting lags 1–3 days, daytime batches.
- Our Drive copies: Cloud Run Job `claimocity-pull` (GCP project ehpa-project-50717, us-east1, service account claimocity-pull@ehpa-project-50717.iam.gserviceaccount.com) runs every 30 min and copies new SFTP files into Drive "Claimocity drop" (1XskJ8BJAC-YQRLRFBZ9lWuyEJiU1-htI) as download__<name>.hl7, one per day about 07:30 UTC. It catches a file only because it runs before the AMD pickup clears it.

## Outage 9/10–9/17 and fix (9/18)
- Every execution since 9/10 died at the Drive login: `google.auth.exceptions.RefreshError: invalid_grant: Token has been expired or revoked` (exit 1). The deployed script used a copy of William's personal Google refresh token from the secret `drive-user-creds`; the token was revoked ~9/10.
- The Claimocity "Password reset" email (admin@claimocity.com, 9/9 10:47 PM ET) was a coincidence: portal/app login, not the SFTP account; SFTP login still works.
- Fix: William shared the Drive folder with the job's service account as Editor and redeployed main.py using `google.auth.default(scopes=[drive])` (code now in repo `claimocity/cloud_run/`). Execution claimocity-pull-j2hvb: `drive folder ok: 12 files already there` / `done: 0 copied, 4 skipped` — nothing newer than 9/9 remains on SFTP (already cleared by the AMD pickup). 9/10–9/17 copies are not recoverable from SFTP; AMD has the charges. Expect download__20260918... in the folder ~07:30Z 9/18 (a send_later check is armed for 09:00Z).
- Never re-run claimocity_redeploy_paste.txt (restores the expired-token version). claimocity_gcs_paste.txt = bucket alternative (ehpa-claimocity-drop, unused); claimocity-reader-key.json = read-only key for that bucket.

## Cloud Shell one-liners (William pastes; Claude sessions cannot reach GCP, and the session credential policy refuses using the reader key)
- describe: `gcloud run jobs describe claimocity-pull --region us-east1 --project ehpa-project-50717 --format="yaml(spec.template.spec.template.spec.containers[0].env,spec.template.spec.template.spec.serviceAccountName)"`
- run: `gcloud run jobs execute claimocity-pull --region us-east1 --project ehpa-project-50717 --wait`
- read THAT run's output in order: `gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_job" AND labels."run.googleapis.com/execution_name"="claimocity-pull-<id>"' --order=asc --project ehpa-project-50717 --format="value(textPayload)"` (without --order=asc the newest entry comes first and `tail` shows the oldest run).
- Reading it: `done: 0 copied, N skipped` = both logins fine, nothing new at Claimocity; `copied ...` = files landed; `UPLOAD FAILED ... 403` = switch to the bucket paste; `Authentication failed` = SFTP password changed -> new version of secret claimocity-sftp-pass. The audit line "failed to complete, 0/1 tasks were a success" has no reason in it.

## Facts for the routine
- The 2-hourly runs only list the Drive folder ("Facesheet folders ... Claimocity ... nothing new"); no run has ever read an HL7 file, and claimocity_in1.py is not in any prompt. Health = newest download__ file ≤ 2 days old (copy job) AND new hospital visits appearing in Claim Inspector (AMD path).
- Proposed prompt v8 line: the 9 AM ET run reports "Claimocity drop: newest file <date>" and flags it when older than 2 days.
- 9/17 Pam admit emails: 5 admits had no AMD chart yet ("Claimocity feed pending") — those charts appear only when the AMD path posts the encounters.
